TS: Thompson Sampling balances exploration and exploitation by sampling from the posterior, which is suitable here as the shortest distance suggests over-exploitation and the model's high lengthscales indicate uncertain regions needing exploration.
UCB: Upper Confidence Bound is chosen to encourage exploration after a recent improvement, addressing the risk of over-exploitation indicated by the very short distance (0.199). The high model lengthscales suggest potential for discovering better minima in under-explored regions, and UCB's exploration bias balances the need to avoid clustering while leveraging remaining iterations.
